We can implement coordinate objective more efficiently:
Given current normalized p (sum=1), c = np.convolve(p,p) (length 2n-1), M = c.max(). Score = 2n*M (since sum=1). For coordinate i:
S0 = 1 - p[i]. If S0 <= small, entire vector is p_i=1? But score ~2n. Not useful but handle.

Coordinate sweep implementation efficient:
```
def _fast_score(p, n):
    c = np.convolve(p,p)
    return 2.0*n*float(c.max())
```

Pseudo for coordinate:
```
L=2*n-1
active_start = i
active_end = min(i+n, L)  # exclusive
# cond
left_max = c[:i].max() if i>0 else -inf
right_max = c[active_end:].max() if active_end<L else -inf
cond=max(left_max, right_max)
idx = np.arange(active_start, active_end)
j = idx - i
center = (idx == 2*i)
old_y = p[i]
term = np.where(center, old_y*old_y, 2*old_y*p[j])
R = c[idx] - term
A = center.astype(np.float64)
B = np.where(center, 0.0, 2.0*p[j])
T0 = S0
# define function scores(X)
def evals(X):
   XX = X*X
   mat = R + (A*XX[None,:] + B*X[None,:])
   M = np.maximum(cond, mat.max(axis=1))
   T = T0 + X
   return 2*n*M/(T*T)
```

2. `greedy_seed(n)`:
```
p = np.zeros(n); p[0]=1.0
for k in range(1,n):
    # contributions from pairs i+j=k with 0<i< <k (and i,j<k) and i≠j? The term involving p_k is 2*p0*p_k (since p0=1). Also if k even, term p_{k/2}^2 where k/2<k and known.
    prev = 0.0
    for i in range(1,k):
        j=k-i
        if 0<j<n:
            if i==j: prev += p[i]*p[j]
            else: prev += 2*p[i]*p[j]
    val = (1.0 - prev) / (2*p0)  # because p0=1, contribution 2p_k
    if val < 0: val = 0
    p[k]=val
return normalize(p)
```
This uses p0=1. Complexity O(n^2) per seed; for n=320 fine. But maybe p values after many k can become negative; set 0. Could also start p0<1? Scale invariant, p0 can be scaled; keeping p0=1 maximizes early endpoint. Maybe also seed with p endpoint at both sides? We can reverse or make symmetric? Symmetric likely worse but still.
Potential Improve: For k, if val too large causing p_k>p_{k-1}? okay.
Could also enforce later constraints by running a simple "projection": After constructing, find max c; scale p by 1/sqrt(max) to reduce peak but scale invariant? Actually objective invariant, scaling doesn't change score. But if peak >1, score still 2n*peak/S^2; local optimization will adjust. Normalizing sum=1 after seed no issue.

3. Power-law seeds:
```
def power_seed(n, alpha, beta, p0=1.0, cap1=0.5? maybe not):
    x = np.arange(n)/max(n-1,1)
    x1 = np.maximum(x, 1e-6)
    w = np.ones(n)
    for i in range(1,n): w[i] = (x1[i])**(-alpha) * (1-x[i])**beta
    # choose scale to set p0=1? Since w[0] singular alpha>0 infinite; set p[0]=1, p[i]=w[i]/w[1] (relative to first)
    p = np.zeros(n); p[0]=1.0; p[1:]=w[1:]/w[1] if w[1]>0
    # but p1 may exceed 0.5; normalize? score invariant, constraint may be peak c0=1; c1 may >1. We can cap p1 at 0.5? c1=2p1; to keep peak at least 1? If p1>0.5, c1>1. We can scale down all? scale invariant but p0 would lower if scale; set p /= sqrt(max(c))? But score invariant. However relative ratios matter; if p1>0.5, local will reduce. Could cap p[i]=min(p[i],0.5/(2?))? Direct cap p1=0.5. But for i>1 early constraint c2=p1^2+2p2; if p2 from cap may >? We'll let local.
    p[:]=np.clip(p,0,1000)
    return normalize(p)
```
But if p1 cap 0.5, p2 maybe large. Could use greedy for early. Maybe combine: use greedy first few and power tail.
Better power seed with relative scaling based on p0=1 and p1=0.5: p_i = 0.5 * (i)^{-alpha} * (1 - i/(n-1))^beta? Then p1=0.5. For alpha=0.5, beta=0 gives p_i=0.5*i^{-0.5}; matches C=0.5? But asymptotic C optimum ~1/√π=0.564, constrained p1=0.5. Local will choose.

- `p[0]=1; p[i]=0.5*(1/i)^alpha*(1 - i/(n-1))**beta` for alpha in [0.3,0.5,0.7], beta in [0,0.2,0.5,1.0]. Normalize.

```
def y_upper(S0, cur_score, n):
    if S0<=0: return 0.0
    denom = 2*n - cur_score
    if denom <= 1e-12: return 1.0
    if cur_score <=0: return 1e-6
    root = (cur_score + math.sqrt(cur_score*(2*n))) / denom
    # root is where 2n*y^2/(S0+y)^2 = cur_score
    return min(1.0, max(1e-12, 1.5*root))  # cap 1?
```

    else p[-1]=1? We can generate from one end and maybe reverse. Use p[0]=1 as one solution; reverse gives same score after local? Reversing optimal same. But seeds with endpoint at last maybe symmetrical; not needed.
    inv_p0=1.0
    for k in range(1,n):
       prev=0.0
       # sum over i=1..k-1; j=k-i; if i<j term 2 p_i p_j; if i=j term p_i^2
       # use loop maybe O(n^2); for n up to 512 okay. Could vectorize for speed:
       for i in range(1,k):
          j=k-i
          if i<j: prev += 2*p[i]*p[j]
          elif i==j: prev += p[i]*p[j]
       val=(1.0-prev)/2.0
       if val<0: val=0.0
       p[k]=val
    return _clean(p)
```

Power seed:
```
def _power_seed(n, alpha, beta):
    if n<2: return np.ones(n)*... 
    x=np.arange(n,dtype=float)
    x_norm=x/(n-1)
    w=np.zeros(n)
    w[0]=1.0
    # to avoid singular at 0, for i>=1 use base=1.0/i; if i=1 base=1
    if n>1:
       base = 1.0/np.maximum(x[1:], 1.0)  # for i=1 base=1
       tail = (1.0 - x_norm[1:])**beta  # at i=n-1 =>0^beta; if beta=0 ->1; if beta<0? no.
       w[1:]=0.5 * base**alpha * tail
    return _clean(w)
```
For beta=0, tail=1. For i=n-1, tail=0; may zero last. okay.
Add symmetric? Not.

def _random_seed(n, rng):
    # decaying random
    p=np.zeros(n); p[0]=1
    for i in range(1,n):
       p[i]=p[i-1]*math.exp(rng.normal(-0.15,0.25))
    p=np.clip(p,0,None)
    return _clean(p)
```

```
def _hi_bound(S0, cur_score, n, old_y):
    if S0<=0: return 1e-12
    denom = 2.0*n - cur_score
    if denom > 1e-9:
        root = (cur_score + math.sqrt(cur_score*(2.0*n))) / denom
        hi = 1.5*root
    else:
        hi = 4.0
    hi = min(hi, 4.0)
    hi = max(hi, old_y, 1e-8)
    return hi
```

def coordinate_refine(p, n, max_sweeps=1000, time_limit=60.0, rng=None):
    start=time.monotonic()
    p=_clean(p)
    if p is None: return p, inf, 0
    score=_fast_score(p,n)
    best_local=score; p_best=p.copy()
    no_improve_sweeps=0
    sweeps=0
    order = None
    while sweeps < max_sweeps and time.monotonic()-start < time_limit:
       sweeps+=1
       improved=0
       # choose order: if rng and sweep%? random else cyclic
       if rng is not None and (sweeps%3==0): order=rng.permutation(n)
       else: order=np.arange(n)
       for i in order:
          if time.monotonic()-start >= time_limit - 2: break
          ... coordinate optimize
          if update: improved +=1; new_score=best_y_score; score=new_score; if score < best_local -1e-12: best_local=score; p_best=p.copy()
       if improved ==0:
          no_improve_sweeps +=1
          if no_improve_sweeps >= max(3, 2*sweeps? no): break
       else: no_improve_sweeps=0
    return p_best, best_local, improved_total? 
```

Helpers:

def _as_vec(x):
    try:
       v = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    except Exception:
       return None
    if v.size == 0: return None
    v = np.nan_to_num(v, nan=0.0, posinf=1000.0, neginf=0.0)
    v = np.clip(v, 0.0, 1000.0)
    return v

def _normalize(v):
    v=_as_vec(v)
    if v is None: return None
    s=float(v.sum())
    if s < 1e-12: return None
    v = v / s
    if v.size < 2: # maybe return? for objective, n=1 valid but our functions handle? coordinate n>=2. Return size 1? Evaluator okay. But for search, if size 1 score 2. We can allow.
       pass
    return v
```
In algorithms require n>=2; if n<2 return [1.0,1.0] fallback.

`_fast_score(v)`:
```
def _fast_score(v):
   v=_as_vec(v)
   if v is None: return inf
   n=v.size
   if n==0: return inf
   s=v.sum()
   if s<1e-12: return inf
   c=np.convolve(v,v)
   m=float(c.max())
   if not math.isfinite(m): return inf
   return float((2.0*n*m)/(s*s))
```
For internal p normalized, use faster no _as_vec. But okay.

```
def _try_update(v, best):
    v=_normalize(v)
    if v is None or v.size < 1: return
    fs=_fast_score(v)
    if not math.isfinite(fs): return
    if fs >= best[0] - 1e-14:
        # maybe still exact if close? no need
        return
    try:
       es=evaluate_sequence([float(x) for x in v])
    except Exception:
       es=fs
    if math.isfinite(es) and es < best[0] - 1e-15:
       best[0]=float(es); best[1]=v.copy()
```

def _greedy_seed(n):
    if n<2: return np.ones(2)/2
    p=np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64); p[0]=1.0
    for k in range(1,n):
        prev=0.0
        # Use loop i=1..k-1
        # We can break if i > k-i? handle double
        for i in range(1,k):
            j=k-i
            if i<j:
                prev += 2.0*p[i]*p[j]
            elif i==j:
                prev += p[i]*p[j]
        val=(1.0 - prev)*0.5
        if val<0: val=0.0
        # cap maybe 0.5? If val>1000? cap
        if val>1000.0: val=1000.0
        p[k]=val
    return _normalize(p)
```
This O(n^2) Python; if n=384, 74k loops, fine. For many seeds maybe 0.2s. We can vectorize `prev` using convolution of p[:k:]? For each k, `conv = np.convolve(p[:k], p[:k])`? c_k of truncated? Since p[:k] indices 0..k-1; full conv coefficient at k? p[0]+? But p[0]=1, we want exclude p0 p_k (unknown) and term p0? Actually current recurrence uses known p1..p_{k-1}. We can compute known = 0.5? Let's derive: c_k = 2 p0 p_k + 2∑_{i=1}^{k-1,i<j} p_i p_j + (if even) p_{k/2}^2. The known sum equals convolution of p[1:k] with itself at coefficient k-1? Let q=p[1:k] length k-1. Convolution q*q at index k-1: ∑_{i=0}^{k-2} q[i] q[k-1-i] = ∑_{i=1}^{k-1} p[i] p[k-i]. This counts each unordered pair once, not doubled. We need 2 for i≠j and 1 for i=j? Actually full convolution c_k known part excluding p0 and p_k: ∑_{i=1}^{k-1} 2 p_i p_{k-i} - (if k even) p_{k/2}^2 because q*q has p_i p_j for ordered i,j: for i≠j both i and j appear, total 2; for i=j one. That is exactly 2 for i≠j, 1 for i=j. So known = conv(q,q)[k-1]. Nice! Let's use vectorized.

q = p[1:k]
if len(q)>0:
   c = np.convolve(q,q)
   prev = float(c[k-1]) if k-1 < len(c) else 0
```

Power seed:
```
def _power_seed(n, alpha=0.5, beta=0.0):
    if n<2: return ...
    idx=np.arange(n,dtype=float)
    x=idx/(n-1)
    p=np.zeros(n)
    p[0]=1.0
    if n>1:
        base=1.0/np.maximum(idx[1:],1.0)  # 1/i
        tail=(1.0 - x[1:])**beta
        if beta == 0: tail = np.ones_like(tail)
        val=0.5 * np.power(base, alpha) * tail
        # If beta>0 and x=1 -> 0, okay.
        p[1:]=val
    return _normalize(p)
```
If alpha=0? base^0=1.
Could ensure p1 <=0.5; val at i=1 =0.5*1*tail(1/(n-1))^beta; if beta large tail small; okay.

Random decaying seed:
```
def _random_seed(n, rng, sigma=0.3, drift=-0.1):
   p=np.zeros(n); p[0]=1.0
   for i in range(1,n):
      p[i]=p[i-1]*math.exp(rng.normal(drift,sigma))
   p=np.clip(p,0,1000)
   return _normalize(p)
```
Maybe values underflow; okay.

LP polish function returns improved p; it may internally do coordinate? We'll just do LP line search, maybe few LPs.
```
def _lp_polish(p,n,time_limit,best):
   t0=time.monotonic(); deadline=t0+time_limit
   p=_normalize(p)
   if p is None or n<4: return p
   for it in range(6):
      if time.monotonic()>deadline: break
      s=p.sum(); c=np.convolve(p,p); M=float(c.max()); score=2n*M/(s*s)
      if score <= 0 or not finite: break
      tl = min(30.0, max(2.0, deadline - time.monotonic()))
      g = _lp_solve(p,M,n,tl)
      if g is None: break
      h=g; if h sum<... break
      # line search t
      b = p.copy(); best_t=p; best_sc=score
      ts=np.linspace(0,1,17)
      for t in ts: cand=(1-t)*b + t*h; cand=_normalize(cand); sc=_fast_score(cand); if sc<best_sc: best_sc=sc; best_t=cand
      # refine t if bounded available and improved
      if minimize_scalar is not None and best_sc < score:
          def ft(t):

`_lp_solve` itself:
```
def _lp_solve(p,M,n,tl):
   if linprog is None or sps is None: return None
   if tl < 1 or M <= 0 or not finite: return None
   q = p / M
   data = _lp_indices(n)
   I,J,row = data
   vals = q[I]
   A = sps.csr_matrix((vals, (row,J)), shape=(2*n-1,n))
   c = -np.ones(n)
   b = np.ones(2*n-1) + 1e-12
   try:
      res=linprog(c, A_ub=A, b_ub=b, bounds=[(0.0,None)]*n, method='highs', options={'presolve':True, 'time_limit': tl})
   except Exception:
      return None
   if res is None or getattr(res,'x',None) is None: return None
   g=res.x
   g=np.asarray(g,float).ravel(); g=np.clip(g,0,None)
   sg=float(g.sum())
   if not finite or sg<=1e-15: return None
   return g/sg
```
Potential memory: `bounds=[(0.0,None)]*n` list of tuples; okay. For speed, use `bounds=(0.0,None)`? SciPy accepts sequence; if single tuple, applies all. Use `bounds=(0.0, None)` maybe. Yes `linprog` accepts bounds=None or iterable. We can use `bounds=(0.0, None)`.

Coordinate function details:
```
def _optimize_coordinate(p, i, c, pref, suff, two_n, current_score):
    n = p.size; s = float(p.sum())
    y = float(p[i]); S0 = s - y
    if S0 <= 1e-15: return None
    # hi bound
    denom = two_n - current_score
    if current_score > 0 and denom > 1e-12:
        u = (current_score + math.sqrt(max(0.0, current_score*two_n))) / denom
        hi = 1.5 * S0 * u
    else:
        hi = 4.0
    hi = max(hi, y, 1e-8)
    hi = min(hi, max(4.0, hi)) # no, ensure at least maybe? if hi < y set y
    # if hi enormous? cap 4 or 10? Use min(hi, 8.0) maybe. But if y>8? normalized p_i≤1 so y≤1 if sum=1. But if s not 1 due? p normalized sum1, y≤1. hi safe cap 8.
    hi = min(hi, 8.0)
```

def scores_grid(X):
   XX=X*X

Coordinate_refine c/pref/suff management:
```
def _coordinate_refine(...):
   start=...
   p=_normalize(p)
   if p is None or p.size<2: return p, inf, 0
   two_n=2.0*n
   c=None; pref=None; suff=None
   best_local=inf; p_best=None
   current_score=None
   total_improved=0
   no_imp_sweeps=0
   for sweep in range(max_sweeps):
      if time.monotonic()-start >= time_limit: break
      improved=0
      if rng is not None and (sweep % 3 == 0): order=rng.permutation(n)
      else: order=np.arange(n)
      for i in order:
         if time.monotonic()-start >= time_limit - 1: break
         if c is None:
            c=np.convolve(p,p)
            s=float(p.sum())
            current_score = two_n*float(c.max())/(s*s)
            pref=np.maximum.accumulate(c)
            suff=np.maximum.accumulate(c[::-1])[::-1]
         res=_optimize_coordinate(p,i,c,pref,suff,two_n,current_score)
         if res is not None:
            best_x,best_sc=res
            # update p
            s=float(p.sum()); old_y=p[i]
            total=s-old_y+best_x
            if total>1e-15:
                p = p.copy(); p[i]=best_x; p /= total
                current_score = best_sc
                improved +=1
                total_improved +=1
                if best_sc < best_local - 1e-12:
                    best_local=best_sc; p_best=p.copy()
                c=None; pref=None; suff=None
            else: ...
      if improved ==0:
         no_imp_sweeps +=1
         if no_imp_sweeps >= 3: break
      else:
         no_imp_sweeps=0
      if best_local < TARGET and no_imp_sweeps: maybe break? No, can still improve.
   if p_best is None: p_best=p; best_local=current_score if finite else _fast_score(p)
   return p_best, best_local, total_improved
```

LP indices cache:
```
_lp_cache={}
def _lp_indices(n):
   if n not in _lp_cache:
      I = np.repeat(np.arange(n, dtype=np.int32), n)
      J = np.tile(np.arange(n, dtype=np.int32), n)
      _lp_cache[n] = (I, J, I+J)
   return _lp_cache[n]
```
If n large, I+J int32 maybe overflow? row up to 1023, no.

def _scores(X):
    X = np.asarray(X, dtype=np.float64)
    XX = X*X
    # If X scalar, make 1d? We'll call 1d.
    base = R[None,:] + B[None,:] * X[:,None]
    base_max = base.max(axis=1)
    M = np.maximum(cond, np.maximum(base_max, Ri + XX))
    T = S0 + X
    return two_n * M / (T*T)
```

def _f(x):

    if cm > m: m = cm
    if cond > m: m = cond
    t = S0 + x
    if t <= 0: return float('inf')
    return two_n*m/(t*t)
```

```
def _active_step(p,n):
   c=np.convolve(p,p); M=float(c.max()); score=2n*M/(s*s)
   active = np.flatnonzero(c >= M - 1e-3*max(M,1e-12))
   if len(active)==0: return p
   g=np.zeros(n)
   for k in active:
      lo=max(0,k-n+1); hi=min(n-1,k)
      j=k-np.arange(lo,hi+1)
      g[lo:hi+1] += p[j]
   g /= len(active)
   g *= 2.0
   # Unconstrained gradient of M; for R, subtract 2M (sum gradient)
   scale = 2.0 * M
   d = g - scale
   # center d to preserve sum in log? subtract mean*d
   d -= d.mean()
   norm = np.linalg.norm(d)
   if norm < 1e-15: return p
   for eta in (0.05,0.1,0.2,0.5):
      q = p * np.exp(-eta * d / (norm/ max(1,n)?))
```

14. `_active_step`: Let's include a simple version.
```
def _active_step(p,n,eta_list=(0.05,0.2,0.8)):
   p=_normalize(p)
   sc0=_fast_score(p); if inf return p
   c=np.convolve(p,p); M=float(c.max()); s=float(p.sum())
   active = np.flatnonzero(c >= M - 1e-4*max(M,1e-300))
   if len(active)==0 or len(active)>2*n-1: return p
   g=np.zeros(n)
   for k in active:
      k=int(k)
      lo=max(0,k-n+1); hi=min(n-1,k)
      # if lo<=hi
      j = k - np.arange(lo, hi+1)
      g[lo:hi+1] += p[j]
   g /= len(active)
   # derivative of M average: 2g? Actually g already sum p_j; derivative = 2*g.
   d = 2.0*g - 2.0*M  # because S=1
   # center
   d -= d.mean()
   amax = float(np.max(np.abs(d)))
   if amax <= 1e-15: return p
   d = d/amax
   best_c=p; best_sc=sc0
   for eta in eta_list:
       q = p * np.exp(-eta * d)
       q = _normalize(q)
       if q is None: continue
       sc=_fast_score(q)
       if math.isfinite(sc) and sc<best_sc-1e-12:
          best_sc=sc; best_c=q
   return best_c
```
This heuristic may increase coordinates where d negative (low active contribution). It could reduce peaks. It uses active set all near M; if many active, d smooth. We can call occasionally.
But note derivative of R has factor 2n; scaling irrelevant. We subtract 2M, not 2M? For S=1, R=2nM, ∂R=2n(∂M - 2M? derivative of M? Wait M is scalar max, but if we perturb p, active M changes. The term -2M ∂(1) because S fixed? If we update multiplicatively and renormalize, sum not fixed during step; but scale invariant, we can ignore sum term? Let's derive direction for R without fixing sum: R=2nM/S^2. gradient = (2n/S^2)(∂M - 2M/S ∂S). For S=1, ∂S=1. ∂M for active set avg derivative = 2g. So gradient =2n(2g - 2M). Our d=2g - 2M. Good. This points to reduce R. Multiplicative with -eta d and normalize approximates. Good.
Could active set include all lags with c near M; if active set large, derivative of M should be convex combination over active lags at local max; using all near M okay.
We can call active_step after coordinate stalls or several times. It is cheap.

def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1300, **kwargs):
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    t0=time.monotonic()

    reserve = 30.0 if bs > 120 else max(3.0, bs*0.1)

    best=[float('inf'), np.array([1.0,1.0], dtype=float)]

Let's write `_clean_vec`:
```
def _clean_vec(x):
    try:
       v = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    except Exception:
       return None
    if v.size == 0:
       return None
    v = np.nan_to_num(v, nan=0.0, posinf=1000.0, neginf=0.0)
    v = np.clip(v, 0.0, 1000.0)
    return v
```
If x is a generator? np.asarray may fail? catch.

`_normalize` returns v/s if s>0. But if v has large 1000s, sum maybe huge, normalize fine.

Now code for `_coordinate_refine`:
```
def _coordinate_refine(p, n, max_sweeps=1000, time_limit=60.0, rng=None, grid_points=41):
    start = time.monotonic()
    p = _normalize(p)
    if p is None:
       return None, float('inf'), 0
    n = int(p.size)
    if n < 2:
       return p, _fast_score(p), 0
    two_n = 2.0 * float(n)
    best_local = _fast_score(p)
    p_best = p.copy()
    c = None; pref=None; suff=None; current_score=best_local
    total_improved=0
    no_imp_sweeps=0
    for sweep in range(int(max_sweeps)):
       if time.monotonic() - start >= time_limit:
          break
       improved=0
       if rng is not None and (sweep % 3 == 0):
          order = rng.permutation(n)
       else:
          order = np.arange(n)
       for i in order:
          if time.monotonic() - start >= time_limit - 1.0:
             break
          if c is None:
             c = np.convolve(p,p)
             s = float(p.sum())
             if s <= 0 or not math.isfinite(s): break
             current_score = two_n * float(np.max(c)) / (s*s)
             if not math.isfinite(current_score): current_score=best_local
             pref = np.maximum.accumulate(c)
             suff = np.maximum.accumulate(c[::-1])[::-1]
          res = _optimize_coordinate(p, i, c, pref, suff, current_score, two_n, grid_points)
          if res is not None:
             best_x, best_sc = res
             s = float(p.sum()); old_y=float(p[i])
             total = s - old_y + best_x
             if total > 1e-15 and math.isfinite(best_x):
                np.copyto? 
                p = p.copy()
                p[i] = best_x
                p /= total
                current_score = best_sc
                improved += 1
                total_improved += 1
                if best_sc < best_local - 1e-12:
                    best_local = float(best_sc)
                    p_best = p.copy()
                c = None; pref = None; suff = None
       if improved == 0:
          no_imp_sweeps += 1
          if no_imp_sweeps >= 3:
             break
       else:
          no_imp_sweeps = 0
    # final ensure p_best maybe better than p
    if p_best is None:
       p_best = p
       best_local = _fast_score(p)
    else:
       # ensure p_best has finite score
       try:
          fs = _fast_score(p_best)
          if math.isfinite(fs) and fs < best_local:
             best_local = fs
       except Exception:
          pass
    return p_best, float(best_local), total_improved
```
If c is None after loop and p_best maybe improved? If improved occurred, p_best copied. If no improved, p_best initial. Good.

`_optimize_coordinate` defined before. It uses `minimize_scalar` global. Need pass `grid_points`.
```
def _optimize_coordinate(p, i, c, pref, suff, current_score, two_n, grid_points=41):
    n = p.size; s = float(p.sum())
    y = float(p[i]); S0 = s - y
    if S0 <= 1e-15 or not math.isfinite(S0):
       return None
    # hi
    if math.isfinite(current_score) and current_score > 0:
       denom = two_n - current_score
       if denom > 1e-12:
          u = (current_score + math.sqrt(max(0.0, current_score * two_n))) / denom
          hi = 1.5 * S0 * u
       else:
          hi = 4.0
    else:
       hi = 4.0
    if not math.isfinite(hi) or hi <= 1e-12:
       hi = max(1e-8, y if y>0 else 1e-6)
    hi = max(hi, y, 1e-8)
    hi = min(hi, 8.0)
    L = 2*n - 1
    Ci = c[i:i+n]
    R = Ci - 2.0*y*p
    R[i] = Ci[i] - y*y
    B = 2.0*p
    B[i] = 0.0
    Ri = float(R[i])
    NEG = -1e300
    left_max = float(pref[i-1]) if i > 0 else NEG
    rs = i+n
    right_max = float(suff[rs]) if rs < L else NEG
    cond = max(left_max, right_max)
    if not math.isfinite(cond): cond = NEG
    # define scores
    def _scores(X):
       X = np.asarray(X, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
       if X.size == 0: return np.array([])
       XX = X*X
       base = R[None, :] + B[None, :] * X[:, None]
       base_max = base.max(axis=1)
       M = np.maximum(cond, np.maximum(base_max, Ri + XX))
       T = S0 + X
       # guard T<=0
       out = np.full(X.size, 1e300)
       mask = T > 1e-300
       out[mask] = two_n * M[mask] / (T[mask]*T[mask])
       return out
    try:
       grid = np.linspace(0.0, hi, int(max(3, grid_points)))
       extra = np.array([0.0, hi, y, 0.5*hi], dtype=float)
       X = np.unique(np.concatenate((grid, extra)))
       X = X[(X >= 0.0) & (X <= hi)]
       if X.size == 0: X = np.array([0.0, hi])
       scX = _scores(X)
       if scX.size == 0 or not np.any(np.isfinite(scX)):
          return None
       scX = np.where(np.isfinite(scX), scX, 1e300)
       bi = int(np.argmin(scX))
       best_x = float(X[bi]); best_sc = float(scX[bi])
       if best_sc >= current_score - 1e-12:
          return None
       # bracket
       if bi == 0:
          left=0.0; right=float(X[1]) if X.size>1 else hi
       elif bi == X.size-1:
          left=float(X[-2]); right=hi
       else:
          left=float(X[bi-1]); right=float(X[bi+1])
       if not (math.isfinite(left) and math.isfinite(right)) or right <= left + 1e-14:
          delta = max(1e-6, 0.1*hi)
          left = max(0.0, best_x - delta)
          right = min(hi, best_x + delta)
       # scalar
       def _f(x):
          x = float(x)
          v = R + B*x
          m = float(v.max())
          cm = Ri + x*x
          if cm > m: m = cm
          if cond > m: m = cond
          t = S0 + x
          if t <= 1e-300: return 1e300
          return two_n * m / (t*t)
       improved_refine=False
       if minimize_scalar is not None:
          try:
             res = minimize_scalar(_f, bounds=(left,right), method='bounded', options={'xatol':1e-10,'maxiter':80})
             if res is not None and hasattr(res,'fun') and math.isfinite(res.fun):
                bx = float(res.x)
                if bx < 0: bx=0.0
                if bx > hi: bx=hi
                fval = float(_f(bx)) if math.isfinite(res.fun) else float(res.fun)
                if math.isfinite(fval) and fval < best_sc - 1e-13:
                    best_sc = fval; best_x=bx; improved_refine=True
              except Exception:
                   pass
       if not improved_refine:
          # fallback local grid
          for _ in range(3):
             if right <= left + 1e-13: break
             Xr = np.linspace(left,right,int(max(9, grid_points//2)))
             Xr = np.unique(np.concatenate((Xr, np.array([best_x]))))
             scr = _scores(Xr)
             scr = np.where(np.isfinite(scr), scr, 1e300)
             b2 = int(np.argmin(scr))
             if scr[b2] < best_sc - 1e-13:
                 best_sc=float(scr[b2]); best_x=float(Xr[b2])
             if b2 == 0:
                 right = float(Xr[1]) if Xr.size>1 else right
             elif b2 == Xr.size-1:
                 left = float(Xr[-2]) if Xr.size>1 else left
             else:
                 left=float(Xr[b2-1]); right=float(Xr[b2+1])
       if best_sc < current_score - 1e-12:
          return (best_x,best_sc)
       return None
    except Exception:
       return None
```

```python
import numpy as np
import time
import math

try:
    from scipy.optimize import linprog, minimize_scalar
except Exception:
    linprog = None
    minimize_scalar = None

try:
    import scipy.sparse as sps
except Exception:
    sps = None

TARGET = 1.50274349
_LP_CACHE = {}


def _clean_vec(x):
    try:
        v = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    except Exception:
        return None
    if v.size == 0:
        return None
    v = np.nan_to_num(v, nan=0.0, posinf=1000.0, neginf=0.0)
    v = np.clip(v, 0.0, 1000.0)
    return v


def _normalize(v):
    v = _clean_vec(v)
    if v is None:
        return None
    s = float(v.sum())
    if not math.isfinite(s) or s < 1e-12:
        return None
    return v / s


def _fast_score(v):
    v = _clean_vec(v)
    if v is None:
        return float("inf")
    n = v.size
    if n == 0:
        return float("inf")
    s = float(v.sum())
    if not math.isfinite(s) or s < 1e-12:
        return float("inf")
    try:
        c = np.convolve(v, v)
        m = float(c.max())
    except Exception:
        return float("inf")
    if not math.isfinite(m):
        return float("inf")
    return float((2.0 * n * m) / (s * s))


def _try_update(v, best):
    v = _normalize(v)
    if v is None or v.size == 0:
        return
    fs = _fast_score(v)
    if not math.isfinite(fs) or fs >= best[0] - 1e-14:
        return
    try:
        es = evaluate_sequence([float(x) for x in v])
    except Exception:
        es = fs
    if math.isfinite(es) and es < best[0] - 1e-15:
        best[0] = float(es)
        best[1] = v.copy()


def _greedy_seed(n):
    n = int(n)
    if n < 2:
        return np.array([1.0, 1.0], dtype=np.float64)
    p = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    p[0] = 1.0
    for k in range(1, n):
        prev = 0.0
        half = k // 2
        for i in range(1, half):
            j = k - i
            prev += 2.0 * p[i] * p[j]
        if k % 2 == 0:
            prev += p[half] * p[half]
        val = 0.5 * (1.0 - prev)
        if val < 0.0:
            val = 0.0
        if val > 1000.0:
            val = 1000.0
        p[k] = val
    return _normalize(p)


def _power_seed(n, alpha=0.5, beta=0.0):
    n = int(n)
    if n < 2:
        return np.array([1.0, 1.0], dtype=np.float64)
    idx = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
    x = idx / float(max(1, n - 1))
    p = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    p[0] = 1.0
    base = 1.0 / np.maximum(idx[1:], 1.0)
    if beta == 0.0:
        tail = np.ones(n - 1, dtype=np.float64)
    else:
        tail = np.power(np.maximum(1.0 - x[1:], 0.0), beta)
    val = 0.5 * np.power(base, alpha) * tail
    p[1:] = val
    return _normalize(p)


def _random_seed(n, rng, sigma=0.35, drift=-0.1):
    n = int(n)
    if n < 2:
        return np.array([1.0, 1.0], dtype=np.float64)
    p = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    p[0] = 1.0
    for i in range(1, n):
        p[i] = p[i - 1] * math.exp(rng.normal(drift, sigma))
    p = np.clip(p, 0.0, 1000.0)
    return _normalize(p)


def _optimize_coordinate(p, i, c, pref, suff, current_score, two_n, grid_points=41):
    n = int(p.size)
    s = float(p.sum())
    y = float(p[i])
    S0 = s - y
    if not math.isfinite(S0) or S0 <= 1e-15:
        return None

    if math.isfinite(current_score) and current_score > 0.0:
        denom = two_n - current_score
        if denom > 1e-12:
            u = (current_score + math.sqrt(max(0.0, current_score * two_n))) / denom
            hi = 1.5 * S0 * u
        else:
            hi = 4.0
    else:
        hi = 4.0

    if not math.isfinite(hi) or hi <= 1e-12:
        hi = max(1e-8, y if y > 0.0 else 1e-6)
    hi = max(hi, y, 1e-8)
    hi = min(hi, 8.0)

    L = 2 * n - 1
    Ci = c[i:i + n]
    R = Ci - 2.0 * y * p
    R[i] = Ci[i] - y * y
    B = 2.0 * p
    B[i] = 0.0
    Ri = float(R[i])

    NEG = -1e300
    left_max = float(pref[i - 1]) if i > 0 else NEG
    rs = i + n
    right_max = float(suff[rs]) if rs < L else NEG
    cond = max(left_max, right_max)
    if not math.isfinite(cond):
        cond = NEG

    def _scores(X):
        X = np.asarray(X, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        if X.size == 0:
            return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
        XX = X * X
        base = R[None, :] + B[None, :] * X[:, None]
        base_max = base.max(axis=1)
        M = np.maximum(cond, np.maximum(base_max, Ri + XX))
        T = S0 + X
        out = np.full(X.size, 1e300, dtype=np.float64)
        mask = T > 1e-300
        out[mask] = two_n * M[mask] / (T[mask] * T[mask])
        return out

    try:
        grid = np.linspace(0.0, hi, int(max(3, grid_points)))
        extra = np.array([0.0, hi, y, 0.5 * hi], dtype=np.float64)
        X = np.unique(np.concatenate((grid, extra)))
        X = X[(X >= 0.0) & (X <= hi)]
        if X.size == 0:
            X = np.array([0.0, hi], dtype=np.float64)

        scX = _scores(X)
        if scX.size == 0 or not np.any(np.isfinite(scX)):
            return None
        scX = np.where(np.isfinite(scX), scX, 1e300)
        bi = int(np.argmin(scX))
        best_x = float(X[bi])
        best_sc = float(scX[bi])

        if best_sc >= current_score - 1e-12:
            return None

        if bi == 0:
            left = 0.0
            right = float(X[1]) if X.size > 1 else hi
        elif bi == X.size - 1:
            left = float(X[-2])
            right = hi
        else:
            left = float(X[bi - 1])
            right = float(X[bi + 1])

        if not (math.isfinite(left) and math.isfinite(right)) or right <= left + 1e-14:
            delta = max(1e-6, 0.1 * hi)
            left = max(0.0, best_x - delta)
            right = min(hi, best_x + delta)

        def _f(x):
            x = float(x)
            v = R + B * x
            m = float(v.max())
            cm = Ri + x * x
            if cm > m:
                m = cm
            if cond > m:
                m = cond
            t = S0 + x
            if t <= 1e-300:
                return 1e300
            return two_n * m / (t * t)

        refined = False
        if minimize_scalar is not None:
            try:
                res = minimize_scalar(
                    _f,
                    bounds=(left, right),
                    method="bounded",
                    options={"xatol": 1e-10, "maxiter": 80},
                )
                if res is not None and hasattr(res, "fun") and math.isfinite(res.fun):
                    bx = float(res.x)
                    bx = min(max(bx, 0.0), hi)
                    fval = float(_f(bx))
                    if math.isfinite(fval) and fval < best_sc - 1e-13:
                        best_sc = fval
                        best_x = bx
                        refined = True
            except Exception:
                refined = False

        if not refined:
            for _ in range(3):
                if right <= left + 1e-13:
                    break
                Xr = np.linspace(left, right, int(max(9, grid_points // 2)))
                Xr = np.unique(np.concatenate((Xr, np.array([best_x], dtype=np.float64))))
                scr = _scores(Xr)
                scr = np.where(np.isfinite(scr), scr, 1e300)
                b2 = int(np.argmin(scr))
                if scr[b2] < best_sc - 1e-13:
                    best_sc = float(scr[b2])
                    best_x = float(Xr[b2])
                if b2 == 0:
                    right = float(Xr[1]) if Xr.size > 1 else right
                elif b2 == Xr.size - 1:
                    left = float(Xr[-2]) if Xr.size > 1 else left
                else:
                    left = float(Xr[b2 - 1])
                    right = float(Xr[b2 + 1])

        if best_sc < current_score - 1e-12:
            return (best_x, best_sc)
        return None
    except Exception:
        return None


def _coordinate_refine(p, n, max_sweeps=1000, time_limit=60.0, rng=None, grid_points=41):
    start = time.monotonic()
    p = _normalize(p)
    if p is None:
        return None, float("inf"), 0
    n = int(p.size)
    if n < 2:
        return p, _fast_score(p), 0

    two_n = 2.0 * float(n)
    best_local = _fast_score(p)
    p_best = p.copy()

    c = None
    pref = None
    suff = None
    current_score = best_local
    total_improved = 0
    no_imp_sweeps = 0

    for sweep in range(int(max_sweeps)):
        if time.monotonic() - start >= time_limit:
            break

        improved = 0
        if rng is not None and (sweep % 3 == 0):
            order = rng.permutation(n)
        else:
            order = np.arange(n)

        for i in order:
            if time.monotonic() - start >= time_limit - 1.0:
                break

            if c is None:
                try:
                    c = np.convolve(p, p)
                except Exception:
                    c = None
                    break
                s = float(p.sum())
                if not math.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
                    break
                current_score = two_n * float(np.max(c)) / (s * s)
                if not math.isfinite(current_score):
                    current_score = best_local
                if current_score < best_local - 1e-12:
                    best_local = float(current_score)
                    p_best = p.copy()
                pref = np.maximum.accumulate(c)
                suff = np.maximum.accumulate(c[::-1])[::-1]

            res = _optimize_coordinate(p, i, c, pref, suff, current_score, two_n, grid_points)
            if res is not None:
                best_x, best_sc = res
                s = float(p.sum())
                old_y = float(p[i])
                tot = s - old_y + best_x
                if tot > 1e-15 and math.isfinite(best_x):
                    p = p.copy()
                    p[i] = best_x
                    p /= tot
                    current_score = float(best_sc)
                    improved += 1
                    total_improved += 1
                    if best_sc < best_local - 1e-12:
                        best_local = float(best_sc)
                        p_best = p.copy()
                    c = None
                    pref = None
                    suff = None

        if improved == 0:
            no_imp_sweeps += 1
            if no_imp_sweeps >= 3:
                break
        else:
            no_imp_sweeps = 0

    fs = _fast_score(p)
    if math.isfinite(fs) and fs < best_local:
        best_local = float(fs)
        p_best = p.copy()

    return p_best, float(best_local), total_improved


def _lp_indices(n):
    if n not in _LP_CACHE:
        I = np.repeat(np.arange(n, dtype=np.int32), n)
        J = np.tile(np.arange(n, dtype=np.int32), n)
        row = I + J
        _LP_CACHE[n] = (I, J, row)
    return _LP_CACHE[n]


def _lp_solve(p, M, n, tl):
    if linprog is None or sps is None:
        return None
    n = int(n)
    if n < 2 or n > 600 or tl < 1.0:
        return None
    if not (math.isfinite(M) and M > 0.0):
        return None

    q = p / float(M)
    I, J, row = _lp_indices(n)
    vals = q[I]
    try:
        A = sps.csr_matrix((vals, (row, J)), shape=(2 * n - 1, n))
        cobj = -np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
        b = np.ones(2 * n - 1, dtype=np.float64) + 1e-12
        res = linprog(
            cobj,
            A_ub=A,
            b_ub=b,
            bounds=[(0.0, None) for _ in range(n)],
            method="highs",
            options={"presolve": True, "time_limit": float(tl)},
        )
    except Exception:
        return None

    if res is None or getattr(res, "x", None) is None:
        return None
    g = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    g = np.nan_to_num(g, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    g = np.clip(g, 0.0, None)
    sg = float(g.sum())
    if not math.isfinite(sg) or sg <= 1e-15:
        return None
    return g / sg


def _lp_polish(p, n, time_limit):
    start = time.monotonic()
    deadline = start + float(time_limit)
    p = _normalize(p)
    if p is None:
        return p
    n = int(p.size)
    if n < 2 or n > 600:
        return p

    for _ in range(6):
        if time.monotonic() >= deadline - 1.0:
            break
        try:
            s = float(p.sum())
            c = np.convolve(p, p)
            M = float(c.max())
        except Exception:
            break
        if not (math.isfinite(M) and M > 0.0) or s <= 0.0:
            break

        tl = max(2.0, min(30.0, deadline - time.monotonic()))
        g = _lp_solve(p, M, n, tl)
        if g is None:
            break
        if sum(float(np.abs(g)) for _ in [0]) < 1e-18:
            break

        h = _normalize(g)
        if h is None:
            break

        base_sc = _fast_score(p)
        if not math.isfinite(base_sc):
            break

        ts = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, 21)
        best_t = 0.0
        best_cand = p.copy()
        best_sc = base_sc
        best_bands = None
        for t in ts:
            cand = (1.0 - t) * p + float(t) * h
            cand = _normalize(cand)
            if cand is None:
                continue
            sc = _fast_score(cand)
            if math.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc - 1e-13:
                best_sc = float(sc)
                best_t = float(t)
                best_cand = cand
                best_bands = int(np.argmin(np.abs(ts - t)))

        if best_sc < base_sc - 1e-10 and minimize_scalar is not None:
            def _blend_score(t):
                cand = (1.0 - float(t)) * p + float(t) * h
                cand = _normalize(cand)
                if cand is None:
                    return 1e300
                sc = _fast_score(cand)
                return sc if math.isfinite(sc) else 1e300

            if best_bands is None:
                ll, rr = 0.0, 1.0
            else:
                idx = int(best_bands)
                if idx == 0:
                    ll, rr = 0.0, float(ts[1]) if len(ts) > 1 else 1.0
                elif idx == len(ts) - 1:
                    ll, rr = float(ts[-2]), 1.0
                else:
                    ll, rr = float(ts[idx - 1]), float(ts[idx + 1])
            try:
                res = minimize_scalar(_blend_score, bounds=(ll, rr), method="bounded",
                                      options={"xatol": 1e-9, "maxiter": 60})
                if res is not None and math.isfinite(res.fun) and res.fun < best_sc - 1e-13:
                    tt = min(max(float(res.x), 0.0), 1.0)
                    cand = _normalize((1.0 - tt) * p + tt * h)
                    if cand is not None:
                        sc = _fast_score(cand)
                        if math.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
                            best_sc = float(sc)
                            best_cand = cand
            except Exception:
                pass

        if best_sc < base_sc - 1e-10:
            p = best_cand
        else:
            break

    return p


def _active_step(p, n, eta_list=(0.05, 0.2, 0.8)):
    p = _normalize(p)
    if p is None:
        return p
    n = int(p.size)
    if n < 2:
        return p
    sc0 = _fast_score(p)
    if not math.isfinite(sc0):
        return p

    try:
        c = np.convolve(p, p)
        M = float(c.max())
    except Exception:
        return p
    if not (math.isfinite(M) and M > 0.0):
        return p

    tol = 1e-4 * max(M, 1e-300)
    active = np.flatnonzero(c >= M - tol)
    if active.size == 0:
        return p

    g = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    for kk in active:
        k = int(kk)
        lo = max(0, k - n + 1)
        hi = min(n - 1, k)
        if lo > hi:
            continue
        j = k - np.arange(lo, hi + 1, dtype=np.int64)
        g[lo:hi + 1] += p[j]
    g /= float(active.size)
    d = 2.0 * g - 2.0 * M
    d -= float(d.mean())
    amax = float(np.max(np.abs(d)))
    if not math.isfinite(amax) or amax <= 1e-15:
        return p
    d /= amax

    best_c = p.copy()
    best_sc = sc0
    for eta in eta_list:
        try:
            q = p * np.exp(-float(eta) * d)
            q = _normalize(q)
            if q is None:
                continue
            sc = _fast_score(q)
            if math.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc - 1e-12:
                best_sc = float(sc)
                best_c = q
        except Exception:
            pass
    return best_c


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1300, **kwargs):
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    try:
        bs = float(budget_s)
    except Exception:
        bs = 1300.0
    if not math.isfinite(bs) or bs <= 0.0:
        bs = 1300.0

    reserve = 35.0 if bs > 150.0 else max(3.0, 0.1 * bs)
    deadline = t0 + max(5.0, bs - reserve)

    def rem():
        return max(0.0, deadline - time.monotonic())

    best = [float("inf"), np.array([1.0, 1.0], dtype=np.float64)]
    _try_update(best[1], best)

    parent = None
    try:
        hp = globals().get("height_sequence_1", None)
        if hp is not None:
            pp = _normalize(hp)
            if pp is not None and pp.size > 1:
                parent = pp
                _try_update(parent, best)
    except Exception:
        parent = None

    sizes = []
    if parent is not None:
        sizes.append(int(parent.size))
    for nn in (320, 256, 384):
        if nn not in sizes:
            sizes.append(nn)
    sizes = [nn for nn in sizes if 2 <= nn <= 512]
    if not sizes:
        sizes = [320]

    candidates = []
    if parent is not None:
        candidates.append((0, parent))

    for rank, nn in enumerate(sizes):
        if rem() < 50.0:
            break
        limit = 5 if rank == 0 else 3
        made = 0
        try:
            gp = _greedy_seed(nn)
            if gp is not None:
                candidates.append((rank + 1, gp))
                made += 1
        except Exception:
            pass
        for alp, bet in ((0.5, 0.0), (0.5, 0.3), (0.5, 0.6), (0.3, 0.0), (0.7, 0.0)):
            if made >= limit:
                break
            try:
                pp = _power_seed(nn, alp, bet)
                if pp is not None:
                    candidates.append((rank + 1, pp))
                    made += 1
            except Exception:
                pass
        if made < limit:
            try:
                rp = _random_seed(nn, rng, sigma=0.35, drift=-0.1)
                if rp is not None:
                    candidates.append((rank + 1, rp))
                    made += 1
            except Exception:
                pass

    candidates = candidates[:12]
    for _, pp in candidates:
        if rem() < 45.0:
            break
        n = int(pp.size)
        if n < 2:
            continue
        tlim = min(12.0, max(3.0, 0.08 * rem()))
        try:
            q, _, _ = _coordinate_refine(pp, n, max_sweeps=10, time_limit=tlim, rng=rng, grid_points=25)
            if q is not None:
                _try_update(q, best)
        except Exception:
            pass

    p = best[1].copy()
    if p.size < 2:
        p = np.ones(16, dtype=np.float64)

    lp_count = 0
    stalls = 0
    last_score = best[0]

    while rem() > 60.0:
        tlim = min(90.0, max(10.0, 0.4 * rem()))
        try:
            q, _, _ = _coordinate_refine(p, p.size, max_sweeps=10000, time_limit=tlim, rng=rng, grid_points=41)
            if q is not None:
                _try_update(q, best)
        except Exception:
            pass
        p = best[1].copy()
        if p.size < 2:
            break

        if best[0] < last_score - 1e-12:
            last_score = best[0]
            stalls = 0
        else:
            stalls += 1

        if rem() > 90.0:
            try:
                q = _active_step(p, p.size)
                if q is not None:
                    _try_update(q, best)
                    p = best[1].copy()
                    if p.size < 2:
                        break
                    if best[0] < last_score:
                        last_score = best[0]
                        stalls = 0
            except Exception:
                pass

        if rem() > 120.0 and lp_count < 10:
            tlim = min(45.0, max(10.0, 0.25 * rem()))
            try:
                q = _lp_polish(p, p.size, tlim)
                if q is not None:
                    _try_update(q, best)
                    p = best[1].copy()
                    if p.size < 2:
                        break
                    if rem() > 60.0:
                        q2, _, _ = _coordinate_refine(p, p.size, max_sweeps=40,
                                                      time_limit=min(30.0, 0.3 * rem()),
                                                      rng=rng, grid_points=41)
                        if q2 is not None:
                            _try_update(q2, best)
                            p = best[1].copy()
                            if p.size < 2:
                                break
                    if best[0] < last_score:
                        last_score = best[0]
                        stalls = 0
            except Exception:
                pass
            lp_count += 1

        if stalls > 6 and rem() > 75.0:
            try:
                sigma = 0.01 + 0.04 * min(1.0, stalls / 5.0)
                q = p * np.exp(sigma * rng.standard_normal(p.size))
                q = _normalize(q)
                if q is not None:
                    qq, _, _ = _coordinate_refine(q, q.size, max_sweeps=8,
                                                  time_limit=min(20.0, 0.2 * rem()),
                                                  rng=rng, grid_points=25)
                    if qq is not None:
                        _try_update(qq, best)
                        p = best[1].copy()
                        if p.size < 2:
                            break
            except Exception:
                pass

            if rem() > 120.0:
                try:
                    n = p.size
                    rq = _random_seed(n, rng, sigma=0.5, drift=-0.1)
                    if rq is not None:
                        qq, _, _ = _coordinate_refine(rq, n, max_sweeps=5,
                                                      time_limit=min(12.0, 0.1 * rem()),
                                                      rng=rng, grid_points=20)
                        if qq is not None:
                            _try_update(qq, best)
                            p = best[1].copy()
                            if p.size < 2:
                                break
                except Exception:
                    pass
            stalls = 0

    if rem() > 20.0:
        try:
            q, _, _ = _coordinate_refine(best[1].copy(), best[1].size, max_sweeps=10000,
                                         time_limit=min(rem() - 10.0, 120.0),
                                         rng=rng, grid_points=65)
            if q is not None:
                _try_update(q, best)
        except Exception:
            pass

    if rem() > 10.0:
        try:
            q = _active_step(best[1].copy(), best[1].size)
            if q is not None:
                _try_update(q, best)
        except Exception:
            pass

    final = best[1]
    final = _normalize(final)
    if final is None or final.size < 2:
        final = np.ones(16, dtype=np.float64)

    try:
        fin_sc = evaluate_sequence([float(x) for x in final])
    except Exception:
        fin_sc = float("inf")

    if not math.isfinite(fin_sc) or fin_sc > best[0] + 1e-7:
        fallback = np.ones(16, dtype=np.float64)
        try:
            fb_sc = evaluate_sequence([float(x) for x in fallback])
        except Exception:
            fb_sc = 2.0
        if math.isfinite(fb_sc):
            final = fallback

    return [float(x) for x in final]
```
